# Requirements trace (Sections 3 to 17)

A65 (RRR-110), audited on main 7ccd1fd9 (2026-10-19 to 2026-10-20). This file traces every concrete feature
requirement in the functional sections of `docs/spec/XOS_4.0_Build_Prompt.md` to the code that implements it.
Section 18 gates are in [gate-status.md](gate-status.md) and Section 20 journeys in
[journey-coverage.md](journey-coverage.md); rows here cite them as evidence where they apply, and do not repeat them.

## Method

- **Extraction.** Each "must", "every", enumerated feature list, settings list, role capability and page list in
  Sections 3 to 17 became one row with a stable id: `S{section}-{nn}` (for example `S4.5.6-20` is the twentieth
  requirement traced in Section 4.5.6). Lists whose items share one implementation (the module pages of Section
  4.5.5, the table names of Section 7.4) are one row; lists whose items stand alone (the 42 org settings) are one
  row each. Paraphrases only; the spec is the authority.
- **Evidence.** Migrations and tables (a fresh `supabase db reset` on a Postgres-only stack), API operations
  (`packages/api/openapi/v1.json`, 1,528 operations, all handled), sitemap routes and the page files behind them,
  pgTAP suites, vitest, Playwright and Maestro journeys, ADRs and owner decisions. A requirement is Met only where
  implementing code was found, and in almost every case a test that exercises it. Names alone were never taken as
  evidence: where a table, component or setting existed but nothing used it, the row says so.
- **Systematic checks** that found most of the gaps:
  - every registered setting was searched for a consumer outside its definition, the settings UI and the
    catalogs (41 of 55 have none, [A65-001](findings/A65-001.md));
  - every Atlas settings section was checked for registered settings or a contributed panel (21 render only an
    empty state, [A65-004](findings/A65-004.md));
  - every `packages/ui` component was checked for use in an app (11 that carry spec behavior are used by none);
  - every notification kind was listed from `private.notification_templates`, and every pg_cron job and Edge
    Function was listed, to see which promised reminders, alerts and workers run at all;
  - every table named in Sections 7.2 to 7.4 was matched against the catalog (equivalent names accepted where the
    Section 7.3 name mapping or an ADR allows them).

## Statuses

- **Met**: implemented, with the evidence named.
- **Partial**: part is implemented; the row names what is missing.
- **Missing**: nothing implements it.
- **Deferred by decision**: an ADR or owner decision defers or replaces it; the row cites it.
- **Blocked on owner**: needs an owner account, secret, decision, hire or paid service.

Rows that another agent is fixing now say "in progress" with the owner: A60 to A62 (visual-review findings
A59-001 to A59-033) and A63 (A54-002, A54-003, A53-001 and the upload staging bucket). Every Partial or Missing
row names its follow-up: an A65 finding, an in-progress finding, a gate, or an owner step.

### What the counts mean

The backend is broadly complete: every Section 7.3 and 7.4 entity has a home (bar ten 1.3 product tables), every
documented operation has a handler, and the Section 7.5 invariants are registered and tested. The gaps cluster in
five places:

1. **Settings that do nothing.** 41 registered settings are stored and audited but read by nothing, among them the
   MFA policy, session lifetime, geofence and photo-on-punch rules, swap rules and the personal accessibility
   preferences ([A65-001](findings/A65-001.md)). 21 settings sections show only an empty state although the
   database and API behind most of them exist ([A65-004](findings/A65-004.md)).
2. **Authentication.** No MFA enrollment, no passkeys, no SAML sign-in, no SCIM endpoint and no OAuth 2.1 server,
   so every step-up check (Restricted export, 1099, break-glass) is unreachable in practice
   ([A65-002](findings/A65-002.md)).
3. **Workers and channels that were never deployed.** The integration worker, the SMS and push senders, the
   saved-search matcher, expiry reminders, the day-90 deletion job and the calendar feed route exist as libraries,
   tables or flags only ([A65-013](findings/A65-013.md), [A65-012](findings/A65-012.md),
   [A65-008](findings/A65-008.md), [A65-015](findings/A65-015.md), [A65-018](findings/A65-018.md)).
4. **Interaction model.** Keyboard navigation and creation, global search, bulk edit, undo, context menus,
   co-editing and live updates are absent or partial in the web shells ([A65-007](findings/A65-007.md),
   [A65-005](findings/A65-005.md), [A65-006](findings/A65-006.md)).
5. **Whole surfaces.** The operator console ([A65-009](findings/A65-009.md)), automations
   ([A65-010](findings/A65-010.md)), Work Details ([A65-003](findings/A65-003.md)) and several Compass features
   ([A65-011](findings/A65-011.md)).

## Small fixes made by A65

- `supabase/tests/1280_doctrine_checks.test.sql`: comments on every table, column and view (Section 7.1), no
  arrays of uuids and a reviewed list of array columns (Section 3.15). Each held on main; nothing guarded them.
- `packages/i18n/test/terminology.test.ts`: the en-US catalog never spells cancelled the British way and never says
  atom (Section 3.6).
- `packages/i18n/REVIEW.md` and `test/review.test.ts`: the review record Section 15 names, with the state of every
  locale.

| S4.3-15 | Notifications: push with deep links, quiet hours, per-category preferences                                                                                                | Met                  | Push sender: the `notification-dispatch` Edge Function sends Push deliveries to Expo with receipts (1361) and to Atlas and Gateway browsers through Web Push (1533); a person's own Compass devices and Gateway browsers (`app.person_push_devices`, 1670) receive the pushes of orgs they work with as a party and of marketplace matches open to them (`private.person_push_fallback`, `app.claim_notification_deliveries`, 1671; ADR 0056). Deep links: the push data carries the record key (1600); every Compass tap, cold start included, opens that record, else its deep link through `compassPathForIncoming`, else the Inbox (`tapRoute`), the Compass Inbox opens notifications the same way (`inboxTarget`), and a Gateway browser push opens the listing or Explore Alerts (`webPushLink`). Quiet hours and category preferences are applied by `private.notify` (0520) and the claim; Compass Settings, Notifications edits the member's category-by-channel matrix with the org's locks, quiet hours in a time zone, the digest and the party categories, queuing the RPCs Atlas and Gateway call (A111, ADR 0071; `apps/compass/src/settings/notifications.ts`, `src/screens/settings/Notifications.tsx`). Tests: pgTAP `1670_person_push_devices`, `1671_person_push_delivery`; vitest `packages/platform/test/push-links.test.ts`, `web-push.test.ts`, `apps/compass/test/notifications.test.ts`, `notification-matrix.test.ts`, `quiet-hours.test.ts`; journey `apps/gateway/e2e/push-reach.spec.ts`; Maestro `push-tap-opens-record`, `settings-notifications` (written, not yet run). Pushes leave only where the Expo and VAPID transports are configured ([A65-012](findings/A65-012.md)) |
| S4.3-16 | Profile: certifications with expiry reminders, documents, emergency contact, uniform sizes                                                                                | Met                  | `apps/compass/app/profile.tsx` with `src/screens/ProfileCredentialsCards.tsx`: certifications (org holder records and profile entries, with issuer, verification and expiry) and documents (signed engagement documents, certificates of insurance, the passport's expiry but never its number) from `api_external.my_profile_certifications` and `my_profile_documents` (2080, ADR 0069), kept offline by the REST downloader; a Public or Internal file opens through a signed URL. Expiry reminders: `private.send_expiry_reminders` sends `certification.expiring` and `document.expiring` at 30, 7 and 1 days on each item's own calendar (1463, A89), and the profile calls out the same thresholds. Emergency contacts and uniform sizes are stored by 1340 (A78), shown on the profile by `src/screens/WorkDetailsCards.tsx` and edited in Compass Settings, Work Details (A111). Security in both manages second factors, recovery codes, passkeys and signed-in sessions (A77, A99; `apps/atlas/components/settings/contributions/a77.tsx` and `a99.tsx`, `apps/gateway/components/settings/security-panels.tsx`). Work Details stores emergency contacts, uniform sizes, dietary and accessibility needs and the travel profile (1340, A78; `contributions/a78.tsx`). Compass lists the sitemap's 14 sections (Keyboard Shortcuts is desktop only, ADR 0006 item 11) and draws all 14 natively (`apps/compass/src/settings/sections.ts` NATIVE_SECTIONS, `apps/compass/app/settings/*.tsx`): the identity group (A110, ADR 0070); Preferences, Accessibility, Notifications, Out of Office and Work Details (A111, ADR 0071); Payout and Tax, Calendar Feeds, Connected Apps and Developer (A112, ADR 0072), through the RPCs Atlas and Gateway call, with changes queued offline. A few steps stay in Gateway on purpose, each with its stated reason in en-US and es-US: adding a security key, step-up with only a security key, creating a company account, giving a tax form, creating a personal API token, and Work Details' passport, loyalty programs, sharing and access log (`apps/compass/test/settings-parity.test.ts`). Uploaded PDFs are stamped page by page with the viewer's email and time on in-app downloads and share links (`packages/data/src/documents/pdf-watermark.ts`, `apps/atlas/lib/files/deliver.ts`; 2040). PNG and JPEG images go out as a tagged PDF carrying the mark (1380), and other classified image types are refused rather than sent unmarked (2040). 2041 hands classified copies only to the web shell holding `XOS_FILE_HANDOVER_SECRET` and blocks direct Storage reads, so until the owner sets the secret those files are refused, never sent unmarked. Tests: `pdf-watermark.test.ts`, `file-delivery.test.ts`, `file-routes.test.ts`, `share-link-file.test.ts`; pgTAP 1380, 2030, 2040 and 2041; journey `a103-pdf-watermarks.spec.ts` (ADR 0061; [A65-016](findings/A65-016.md) Fixed) |

## Section 9: Security requirements

| ID    | Requirement                                                                                                       | Status           | Evidence or gap                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| S9-01 | Email with password and magic link                                                                                | Met              | `signInWithPassword`, `signInWithOtp` in `apps/atlas/app/auth/actions.ts` and Gateway                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| S9-02 | Passkeys (WebAuthn)                                                                                               | Met              | Passkey sign-in on the Atlas and Gateway sign-in pages, and adding, renaming and removing passkeys on Personal settings, Security (A77, `apps/atlas/components/auth/security`, `apps/gateway/components/auth/security`; `packages/data/test/auth-assurance.test.ts`). Supabase Auth holds the passkeys and WebAuthn challenges (`[auth.passkey]` and `[auth.webauthn]` in `supabase/config.toml`, hosted values in `infra/runbooks/hosted-security-settings.md`). Compass does not offer passkeys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| S9-03 | Google, Apple and Microsoft OAuth                                                                                 | Partial          | Google and Microsoft from `OAUTH_PROVIDERS` in Atlas and Gateway (`apps/atlas/lib/auth/providers.ts`, `apps/gateway/lib/auth/providers.ts`), offered when configured. Apple signs in natively in Compass on iOS (ADR 0033 decision 58); Atlas and Gateway leave it out until its web flow, with Apple's button, CSP origin and six-month client secret, is built (ADR 0036, amendment of 2026-10-23, follow-ups). Provider credentials are owner configuration                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| S9-04 | Enterprise SAML SSO and SCIM provisioning                                                                         | Met              | SAML sign-in from `/auth/sso` with just-in-time join, and SCIM provisioning through the `scim` Edge Function (A77, 1332, pgTAP 1332); `saml_sso` and `scim` plan gates. SAML is tested only against a hosted project, and the Supabase SAML add-on is enabled when the first Enterprise org needs it (ADR 0033 decision 71)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| S9-05 | Passwords checked against breached-password lists                                                                 | Blocked on owner | Supabase leaked-password protection on Pro (ADR 0033 decision 3)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| S9-06 | MFA with TOTP and passkeys; orgs enforce MFA per role                                                             | Met              | Authenticator apps (TOTP) and security keys or passkeys as second factors: enrollment on Personal settings, Security and the challenge on `/auth/mfa` in Atlas and Gateway, and TOTP verification in Compass (A77; `apps/atlas/e2e/a77-auth.spec.ts`, `apps/compass/test/assurance.test.ts`). `security.mfa_policy` (optional, admins or everyone) is read against the caller's roles by `private.mfa_required` and `app.my_org_auth_status` (1330, pgTAP 1330) and enforced by the Atlas proxy, the API runtime and Compass, not on direct calls to the Supabase endpoint (ADR 0033 decision 55)                                                                                                                                   |
| S9-07 | Session lifetime configurable per org                                                                             | Met              | `security.session_hours` ends a session older than the org allows in the Atlas proxy, the API runtime and Compass, and `security.idle_timeout_minutes` ends an idle Atlas session, judged from a last-seen cookie (`app.my_org_auth_status`, 1330, pgTAP 1330; `apps/atlas/test/assurance.test.ts`, `packages/api/test/auth.test.ts`, `apps/compass/test/assurance.test.ts`). Neither applies to direct calls to the Supabase endpoint (ADR 0033 decision 55)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| S9-08 | Step-up for billing, data export, role changes and API key creation                                               | Met              | Step-up (`private.require_second_factor`, 1333) for Restricted exports, the 1099 file, SCIM tokens, break-glass, role changes and API key creation (1562, pgTAP 1562, Atlas step-up dialog), and for billing: Checkout and the customer portal ask for it in `app.begin_billing_checkout`, `app.record_billing_checkout` and `app.billing_portal_customer` (1640, pgTAP 1640; ADR 0033 decision 59); restrictive policies keep it on the subscription tables (ADR 0055 decision 13)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| S9-09 | Keys in Vault and Vercel encrypted variables; CI secret scanning                                                  | Met              | Vault (ADR 0024 item 1); gitleaks `secrets` job (`.gitleaks.toml`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| S9-10 | Org IP allow-lists cover Atlas and the API                                                                        | Met              | 1201, API authenticator; the direct Supabase endpoint limit is accepted for launch (ADR 0033 decisions 33 and 55, ADR 0034 item 5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| S9-11 | TLS 1.2+, HSTS preload; AES-256 at rest                                                                           | Met              | `apps/atlas/lib/security/headers.ts` HSTS with preload; platform encryption at rest                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| S9-12 | Field-level pgcrypto for tax identifiers, bank details, government identifiers and medical notes                  | Met              | Field-level pgcrypto under its own Vault key per field: tax identifiers (0730, 1200), medical notes (0363), passport numbers (1340), vendor bank account and routing numbers (2010: written only through `app.set_vendor_bank_account`, decrypted only in `app.vendor_bank_accounts_masked` for `data.restricted.read.banking`) and supplier diversity certificate numbers (2011 and 2350: masked to the last four, with an audited reveal that asks for step-up and a reason, decision 38). Payee bank details stay with the provider (`payout_accounts`). pgTAP 2010 and 2011, where 2011 checks that no Restricted banking or government identifier column anywhere stores plain text (ADR 0057; [A65-003](findings/A65-003.md)) |
| S9-13 | OWASP ASVS 5.0 Level 2 mapped in `legal/security/asvs-matrix.md`                                                  | Met              | `legal/security/asvs-matrix.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| S9-14 | Strict CSP with nonces; same-site cookies; CSRF protection on server actions                                      | Met              | `createNonce` in both proxies; `__Host-xos-auth` cookie and Origin check (ADR 0026 security item 6)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| S9-15 | SSRF protection on webhooks and imports via an allow-list resolver                                                | Met              | `packages/platform/src/webhooks/dispatch.ts`; imports fetch no URLs (Google Sheets import is absent)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Signed commits on main are a repository setting (owner)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## Section 14: Compliance

The legal artifact rows are tracked in `legal/compliance-matrix.md`, whose states still read "Planned (Wave n)" from earlier waves. The rows below record product evidence.

| S17-09 | Carrier tracking for UPS, FedEx, DHL on shipments                                                   | Partial | `connectors/shipping`, 0634, 1021 (pgTAP 0634, 1021); shipments start and stop tracking through a Connected carrier (`logistics-hospitality/tracking.tsx`), and the `integration-worker` Edge Function (A83, ADR 0039) runs the UPS, FedEx and DHL connectors on a Tracking Refresh run. UPS and FedEx mint access tokens with the OAuth 2.0 client-credentials grant from the connection's client ID and secret, cached per connection until five minutes before expiry and refreshed once after a 401, with credentials cut from every error (A105, ADR 0065; `packages/integrations/test/shipping.test.ts`); DHL keeps its API key. Nothing schedules Tracking Refresh runs: a tracker updates only when someone selects Refresh tracking and Sync now on the connection. Carrier apps are an owner step (`packages/integrations/README.md`) ([A65-013](findings/A65-013.md)) |
